# Academy Award for Best Production Design

Academy Award which recognizes achievement for art direction in film

| Academy Award for Best Production Design |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Country                                  | United States                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Presented by                             | [Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_of_Motion_Picture_Arts_and_Sciences "Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences") (AMPAS)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| First awarded                            | [1927](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1st_Academy_Awards "1st Academy Awards")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Most recent winner                       | [James Price](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Price_\(art_director\) "James Price (art director)") [Shona Heath](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shona_Heath "Shona Heath") [Zsuzsa Mihalek](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zsuzsa_Mihalek "Zsuzsa Mihalek") *[Poor Things](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Poor_Things_\(film\) "Poor Things (film)")* ([2023](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/96th_Academy_Awards "96th Academy Awards")) |

The **Academy Award for Best Production Design** recognizes achievement for [art direction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Art_direction "Art direction") in [film](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Film "Film"). The category's original name was **Best Art Direction**, but was changed to its current name in 2012 for the [85th Academy Awards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/85th_Academy_Awards "85th Academy Awards").[\[1\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Music_Rules-1) This change resulted from the Art Directors' branch of the [Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_of_Motion_Picture_Arts_and_Sciences "Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences") (AMPAS) being renamed the Designers' branch. Since [1947](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/19th_Academy_Awards "19th Academy Awards"), the award is shared with the set decorators. It is awarded to the best interior design in a film.[\[2\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-2)

The films below are listed with their production year (for example, the [2000 Academy Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/72nd_Academy_Awards "72nd Academy Awards") for Best Art Direction is given to a film from 1999). In the lists below, the winner of the award for each year is shown first, followed by the other nominees in alphabetical order.

| --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Most Awards                             | [Cedric Gibbons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cedric_Gibbons "Cedric Gibbons")    | 11 awards      | Awards resulted from 39 nominations.[\[3\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-theoscarsite-3)[\[4\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-artdesign-4) |
| Most Nominations                        |                                                                                    | 39 nominations | Nominations resulted in 11 awards.                                                                                                                                |
| Most Nominations (without ever winning) | [Roland Anderson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roland_Anderson "Roland Anderson") | 15 nominations | Nominations resulted in no awards.[\[4\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-artdesign-4)                                                                   |

## Notes

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design\&action=edit\&section=14 "Edit section: Notes")]

1. **[^](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_ref-twentynine_5-0)** The 2nd Academy Awards is unique in being the only occasion where there were no official nominees. Subsequent research by AMPAS has resulted in a list of unofficial or de facto nominees, based on records of which films were evaluated by the judges.
2. **[^](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_ref-forty_6-0)** Prior to 1941, only credited art directors and assistant art directors were eligible for nomination.
3. **[^](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_ref-sishopkins_7-0)** [Republic Pictures](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republic_Pictures "Republic Pictures") submitted *[Sis Hopkins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sis_Hopkins_\(1941_film\) "Sis Hopkins (1941 film)")* and it was initially named as a nominee. However, the studio later withdrew the film from consideration and it is not considered an official nominee.
4. **[^](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_ref-openroad_9-0)** [United Artists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Artists "United Artists") submitted *[Song of the Open Road](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Song_of_the_Open_Road "Song of the Open Road")* and it was initially named as a nominee. However, the studio later withdrew the film from consideration and it is not considered an official nominee.
5. ^ [***a***](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_ref-fiftyseven_11-0) [***b***](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_ref-fiftyseven_11-1) In 1957 and 1958, black-and-white and color films competed in a combined Best Art Direction category.

## Shortlisted finalists

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design\&action=edit\&section=15 "Edit section: Shortlisted finalists")]

Finalists for Best Production Design were selected by branch members, who voted for ten finalists which were screened to determine the five nominees.[\[31\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Champlin-36)

| Year | Finalists                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Ref                                                            |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
